A prediction matches an annotation <sub>on</sub>l<sub>y</sub> if it <sub>uses</sub> th<sub>e same source un</sub>it <sub>an</sub>d <sub>preserves</sub> th<sub>e anno-</sub> t<sub>a</sub>t<sub>e</sub>d b<sub>e</sub>h<sub>av</sub>i<sub>or or accep</sub>t<sub>ance con</sub>diti<sub>on.</sub> E<sub>ac</sub>h <sub>anno</sub>t<sub>a</sub>ti<sub>on may</sub> b<sub>e ma</sub>t<sub>c</sub>h<sub>e</sub>d <sub>once;</sub> d<sub>up</sub>li<sub>ca</sub>t<sub>es an</sub>d <sub>unma</sub>t<sub>c</sub>h<sub>e</sub>d <sub>pre</sub>di<sub>c</sub>ti<sub>ons</sub> d<sub>o</sub> not <sup>i</sup>ncrease covera<sub>g</sub>e:

$$
\mathrm { C o v } _ { \mathrm { d e v } } ( c ) = \frac { \left| \left\{ d \in \mathcal { D } _ { \mathrm { d e v } } : \exists \hat { d } \in \widehat { \mathcal { D } } _ { c } , \mathrm { M a t c h } ( \hat { d } , d ) \right\} \right| } { \left| \mathcal { D } _ { \mathrm { d e v } } \right| } .\tag{14}
$$

F<sub>or an ex</sub>t<sub>rac</sub>t<sub>e</sub>d di<sub>rec</sub>ti<sub>on</sub> $\hat { d } ,$ Acct $( \hat { d } )$ <sub>requ</sub>i<sub>res an exp</sub>li<sub>c</sub>it di<sub>spos</sub>iti<sub>on an</sub>d <sub>a nonemp</sub>t<sub>y eva</sub>l<sub>ua</sub>ti<sub>on prov</sub>id<sub>er an</sub>d <sub>cr</sub>it<sub>e-</sub> rion. Mapped (u) holds when source unit u grounds a direction or is explicitly marked <sub>a</sub>d<sub>v</sub>i<sub>sory, a</sub>b<sub>s</sub>t<sub>a</sub>i<sub>ne</sub>d<sub>, or res</sub>id<sub>ua</sub>l<sub>:</sub>

$$
\mathrm { A c c o u n t e d } ( c ) = \frac { \sum _ { \hat { d } \in \widehat { \mathcal { D } } _ { c } } \mathbf { 1 } [ \mathrm { A c c t } ( \hat { d } ) ] } { | \widehat { \mathcal { D } } _ { c } | } ,
$$

$$
\mathrm { U n i t M a p } ( c ) = \frac { \sum _ { u \in { \mathcal { U } } _ { \mathrm { d e v } } } \mathbf { 1 } [ \mathrm { M a p p e d } _ { c } ( u ) ] } { | \mathcal { U } _ { \mathrm { d e v } } | } ,\tag{15}
$$

$$
\mathrm { V a l i d R e c } ( c ) = \frac { \sum _ { \hat { d } \in \widehat { \mathcal { D } } _ { c } } \mathbf { 1 } [ \mathrm { V a l i d } ( \hat { d } ) ] } { | \widehat { \mathcal { D } } _ { c } | } .
$$
